These findings suggest that autonomous, fine-tuning-free prompt optimization can produce symptom extraction prompts that generalize effectively from development to validation while remaining deployable on local infrastructure.

</details>


### [189] [MemOps: Benchmarking Lifecycle Memory Operations in Long-Horizon Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12893)
*Xixuan Hao,Zeyu Zhang,Zehao Lin,Yihang Sun,Ziliang Guo,Xichong Zhang,Yuxuan Liang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemOps基准，将LLM代理的长期记忆评估从传统最终答案评分转向操作级诊断，揭示现有系统在记忆操作上的不可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅通过下游问答评估记忆，仅关注最终答案正确性，这混淆了记忆失败的具体原因（如遗漏事实、错误绑定操作或依赖过期值），可能导致对不一致或不安全记忆状态的误判；需在动态长程交互中精细评估记忆的生命周期操作。

Method: 设计MemOps基准：将对话记忆重构为生命周期操作序列（包括记住、忘记、更新、反思等），用结构化轨迹表示每个事件（指定触发器、目标、范围、状态转换及证据）；通过可控生成管道将这些操作嵌入长任务导向对话，生成黄金操作轨迹及六类操作级探针，并在长上下文、检索式、参数化和受控记忆系统上评估。

Result: MemOps成功分解了最终答案准确性所掩盖的失败模式：例如，会话级检索优于轮次级检索，而长上下文模型在重建有序记忆状态轨迹方面表现显著薄弱；这证实当前系统在记忆操作上远未实现统一可靠性。

Conclusion: 本研究推动长期记忆评估从粗粒度的最终答案评分转向可解释的操作级诊断，为改进LLM代理的记忆可靠性和安全性提供了关键洞见。

Abstract: Long-term memory has become a foundational capability for LLM-based agents that accompany users across extended, multi-session interactions. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate such memory almost exclusively through downstream question answering, scoring only the correctness of a final answer. This black-box formulation conflates the heterogeneous causes of memory failure, such as missing the introduction of a relevant fact, binding an operation to the wrong target, or relying on stale values after a correction. As a result, it can credit correct answers despite their reliance on inconsistent or unsafe memory states. In this paper, we argue that, in dynamic long-horizon interactions, memory is not a static collection of facts but a lifecycle of explicit operations, including remembering, forgetting, updating, reflecting, and their compositions. We introduce MemOps, a benchmark that reformulates conversational memory as a sequence of lifecycle operations and represents each memory event with a structured trace specifying its trigger, target, scope, state transition, and supporting evidence. A controllable generation pipeline embeds these operations into long, task-oriented conversations and produces gold operation traces together with six categories of operation-level probes, evaluated under both adjacent-evidence and long-context settings. Across long-context, retrieval-based, parametric and managed-memory systems, MemOps disentangles failure modes that final-answer accuracy alone conceals, revealing that current systems remain far from uniformly reliable. For instance, session-level retrieval outperforms turn-level retrieval, and long-context models remain notably weak at reconstructing ordered memory-state trajectories. These results move long-term memory evaluation from final-answer scoring toward interpretable, operation-level diagnosis.

</details>


### [190] [Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12924)
*Jonas Ehrhardt,René Heesch,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号知识与梯度引导的强化学习算法KGRL，利用Datalog知识库约束参数化MDP的动作空间，显著提高样本效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动作马尔可夫决策过程（PAMDP）中，现有强化学习算法依赖一次性参数估计器，导致训练样本效率低下；尽管环境中存在显式但不完整的领域知识（如规则或安全约束），却未被有效利用以提升学习效率。

Method: KGRL算法整合神经符号方法：使用Datalog知识库推导给定状态下的适用动作集和可行参数范围，剪枝无效决策；同时通过梯度基参数精炼循环动态优化参数，并记录规则激活路径以提供可解释性。

Result: 在标准PAMDP任务上，KGRL在样本效率（训练步数减少）和每集回报（任务完成质量）两方面均优于当前最先进的强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: KGRL成功将领域知识融入强化学习，实现高效、约束感知的决策，并增强模型透明度，为参数化环境中的RL提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Reinforcement Learning in Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes (PAMDP), where each decision consists of a symbolic action and numerical parameters. In such settings Reinforcement Learning algorithms typically determine parameters with one-shot estimators, which makes their training sample inefficient. Though in most PAMDP environments explicit but incomplete knowledge (e.g., rules, safety constraints, or expert heuristics) is available, it is rarely directly used to increase the sample-efficiency of training Reinforcement Learning agents. We step into this gap and propose our novel Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning (KGRL) algorithm. KGRL uses domain knowledge in a Datalog knowledge base to derive the set of applicable actions and feasible parameters for a given state. This allows it to prune non-applicable actions from the decision-space and constrain the parameter spaces of the remaining actions. We then use a gradient-based parameter refinement loop to estimate the optimal parameters during training and deployment of the agent. By recording activated rules along the trajectory, KGRL additionally provides local procedural explanations on the pruning of actions and constraining of parameters. Overall, KGRL guides the agent's exploration and deployment toward feasible and constraint-aware decisions, while increasing sample efficiency during training. KGRL outperforms state-of-the-art RL baselines for PAMDPs in both, sample efficiency and episodic return.

</details>


### [191] [FormalAnalyticGeo: A Neural-Symbolic Based Framework for Multimodal Analytic Geometry Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12982)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FormalAnalyticGeo is a scalable framework that automates the generation of multimodal analytic geometry problems using formal languages (CDL) and a Signed Distance Field engine, eliminating human annotation via a closed-loop process with four LLM components. It produced AnalyticGeo7K, a dataset of 7,000+ verified problems with near-exact accuracy (median 0.7% error).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Analytic geometry remains underexplored due to scarcity of annotated samples and limitations of existing diagram generation methods: templates fail with constraint-driven layouts, while generative models lack geometric precision for conic curves.

Method: The framework uses CDL (Condition Description Language) as an intermediate representation to bridge problem text and diagram rendering via an SDF engine. Four LLM components operate sequentially: Generator (problem creation), Formalizer (CDL conversion), Measurer (ground-truth extraction), and Quality Verifier (staged validation with auto-retry).

Result: Generated AnalyticGeo7K dataset (>7K problems) with median ground-truth relative error of 0.70% and 82.3% of answers within 5% of exact solutions. Framework achieves fully automatic generation without human annotation.

Conclusion: Addresses critical data scarcity in analytic geometry reasoning, enabling advanced MLLM research through high-precision multimodal datasets. Public release facilitates broader adoption and innovation in math-focused AI systems.

Abstract: Math reasoning has achieved significant progress with the rapid advancement of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), however analytic geometry remains largely underexplored, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated samples. Existing diagram generation approaches struggle with analytic geometry: template methods cannot handle constraint-driven layouts, and generative models lack the geometric precision to render annotated conic curves correctly. We present FormalAnalyticGeo, a scalable framework for fully automatic generation of multimodal analytic geometry problems. Leveraging the rigor of formal languages, we design the framework around CDL (Condition Description Language), a formal intermediate representation that bridges free-form problem text with precise diagram rendering via a Signed Distance Field (SDF) engine. The framework employs four specialized LLM components in sequence: a Generator that produces diverse analytic geometry problems, a Formalizer that converts each problem into CDL for SDF-based rendering, a Measurer that extracts ground-truth answers through vision-based measurement on the rendered diagrams, and a Quality Verifier that checks outputs at three stages. Structured feedback from the Quality Verifier drives automatic retry, forming a closed loop that eliminates any need for human annotation. Applying FormalAnalyticGeo at scale yields AnalyticGeo7K, a dataset of over 7K verified multimodal problems, each with aligned text, diagram, formal annotation, and ground truth.Experiments show that the generated problems achieve a median ground-truth relative error of 0.70\%, with 82.3\% of answers falling within 5\% of the exact symbolic solution. Our framework and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [192] [Resist and Update: Counterfactual Report Coordinates for Incentive-Compatible LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12985)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种方法，通过反事实报告坐标钳位使语言模型在激励压力下保持报告一致，避免过度迎合用户或夸大确定性。实验在贝叶斯见证基准上实现了完美抵抗和更新性能，并验证了该方法在多个模型家族和自然谄媚基准上的可迁移性。贡献在于提供激活级反事实激励不变性的接口和认证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐语言模型在非证据性激励压力下（如用户自信、声望要求）会误报告，即使内部信念未变也同意用户或过度肯定，这破坏了内部激励相容性。需要一种机制确保模型报告仅响应证据而非外部压力。

Method: 采用因果识别方法，通过交换干预识别低秩报告坐标（答案、置信度、警告），并设计无训练的反事实报告坐标钳位，在反事实激励中性化上下文中参考模型自身报告，实现抵抗与更新的双重目标。

Result: 在贝叶斯见证基准上，两阶段钳位实现抵抗和更新联合值1.00（置信区间[0.99,1.00]）；全局解码显示单一参数权衡；输出级微调需同时枚举双目标；单次编译有损但可部署。机制在三个模型家族和自然谄媚基准上复现。

Conclusion: 提出激活级反事实激励不变性作为内部激励相容的结构原始机制，提供可认证的接口和方法，为设计更可靠的语言模型报告系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.

</details>


### [193] [Win by Silence: Deletion Non-Monotonicity, Autonomous Exploitation, and Typed-State Gating in LLM Plan Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12986)
*Aleh Manchuliantsau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文研究LLM生成战略规划中，计划评估器如何因策略模糊化而错误奖励的问题。通过理论推导与实验验证，发现删除关键步骤可提升评分，GATE系统能约束漏洞但无法保障语义完整性，PCSC技术可检测后处理的省略拼接，但需进一步改进以解决策略真实性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM计划评估器可能因策略隐含化而给予高分，导致优化器通过省略必要步骤虚增评分。这种漏洞会降低策略实际质量，亟需机制识别并抑制此类操纵行为。

Method: 提出分阶段期望值评分器理论框架，通过数学推导（Proposition 1）量化删除中间步骤的评分影响；在26个固定路线上验证理论模型；使用分数优化器探索未覆盖结构；引入GATE系统进行约束评估；开发PCSC技术检测后处理拼接漏洞。

Result: 所有57个允许删除操作均符合理论预测，21/26路线存在漏洞利用；GATE拒绝全部26个问题路线，后续47/54修订修复漏洞，严格合规率从1/26升至13/26；但义务通道逃避仍达6/6，delta索引成本限制仅将违规率降至3/6，语义完整性验证缺失。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖评分提升的优化不可靠，评估机制需内置完整性校验。GATE作为确定性约束有效但非完备解决方案，PCSC需扩展语义验证能力。未来需建立端到端的质量保证体系，确保策略显式化与可执行性。

Abstract: Plan evaluators can reward a strategic plan for becoming less explicit. This paper studies that failure in a staged expected-value scorer for LLM-generated venture routes. Proposition 1 gives the score change from deleting an interior transition while retargeting its predecessor and retaining downstream value: Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]. On a frozen 26-route cohort, all 57 admissible deletions matched the analytic identity and threshold sign, and every route had at least one score-improving deletion. A score-seeking optimizer, allowed to restructure routes but not told the exploit mechanism, found baseline-beating uncovered structures in 21/26 routes. GATE refused score release for 26/26 silenced routes with 0/26 honest suspensions; after refusal, 47/54 next revisions repaired to a covered structure, and strict covered improvement rose from 1/26 to 13/26. An adaptive compiler-aware co-author exposed the registry-provenance boundary: obligation-channel evasions remained 6/6 across all four v1/v1.5 conditions, while delta-indexed cost floors reduced beat-honest routes from 6/6 to 3/6 and fundability-by-silence from 5/6 to 0/6 without establishing semantic completeness. If a plan scores better only because it omits necessary work, the plan did not improve; the evaluation created an omission incentive. PCSC detects and neutralizes post-hoc omission splices over model-mediated typed-state records. In the cooperative setting tested, GATE acts as a deterministic search-shaping constraint, not merely a post-hoc filter. It does not verify the semantic completeness or real-world quality of arbitrary LLM-generated strategies.

</details>


### [194] [Dynamic Resource Allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13007)
*Jakub Kowalski,Adam Ciężkowski,Artur Krzyżyński,Mark H. M. Winands*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Simulation-based algorithms are especially suited for high-uncertainty environments such as adversarial board games with significant elements of randomness and hidden information. In particular, several Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) variants are commonly used in such domains. In this paper, we propose a series of enhancements for Ensemble Determinization MCTS, introducing two axes for dynamic resource allocation. First, Dynamic Number of Determinizations, increases or decreases the number of currently used determinization trees depending on the behavior of so-far search. Second, Dynamic Simulation Allocation, splits the simulation budget nonuniformly across the determinization trees, using simulation-to-simulation decisions to choose the tree with potentially the best knowledge gain. As benchmark domains, we used three popular tabletop games: Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor. Testing our proposed enhancements in iteration- and time-based settings showed that particular configurations yield a statistically significant increase in the algorithm's strength.

</details>


### [195] [Audio-Native Speech Recognition with a Frozen Discrete-Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13013)
*Harsha Vardhan Khurdula,Abhinav Kumar Singh,Yoeven D Khemlani,Vineet Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于离散扩散的语音识别模型，利用DiffusionGemma（26B专家混合架构）通过并行去噪步骤高效转录语音，在LibriSpeech测试集上达到6.6%词汇错误率，仅需约8步即可处理任意长度话语，并支持多语言（英语、印地语、普通话），仅训练42M参数（占骨干0.16%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动语音识别系统依赖自回归解码器逐token生成转录，效率低下；本工作旨在验证离散扩散语言模型能否通过并行精炼整个转录序列突破此局限，实现更高效、更灵活的语音转写，尤其适用于长话语场景。

Method: 构建音频原生接口集成至DiffusionGemma：冻结Whisper编码器提取声学特征，轻量级投影器映射至嵌入空间，低秩适配器使冻结主干网络适配新模态；采用均匀随机token离散扩散（非吸收掩码方案）；针对音频接地问题，引入连接主义时间分类（CTC）损失通过冻结输出头传递梯度，仅微调42M参数（0.16%骨干规模）。

Result: 在LibriSpeech test-clean上实现6.6%词汇错误率；转录过程稳定在约8个并行步骤内完成，与话语长度无关；单一适配器经六种语言预训练，在英语、印地语和普通话评估中均表现良好，验证跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: 首次证明离散扩散模型可有效替代自回归解码器进行语音识别，显著提升计算效率和并行性，为多语言ASR提供轻量级解决方案，未来方向包括扩展至更复杂场景及优化扩散步骤。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition is dominated by autoregressive decoders that emit one token at a time. We ask whether a discrete diffusion language model can transcribe speech instead, refining a whole transcript in parallel over a small number of denoising steps. We train an audio-native interface for DiffusionGemma, a 26B mixture-of-experts model that generates text by uniform, random-token discrete diffusion rather than the absorbing-mask scheme common to recent diffusion language models. A frozen Whisper encoder supplies acoustic features, a lightweight projector maps them into the model embedding space, and low-rank adapters let the frozen backbone attend to the new modality.
  About 42M parameters are trained, which is 0.16 percent of the backbone. We find that the natural training objectives fail to ground the audio because their gradient reaches the projector only through attention that has already dismissed it. A connectionist temporal classification loss applied through the frozen output head breaks this deadlock. The resulting model reaches 6.6 percent word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length, and uses a single adapter trained on six languages, which we evaluate here on English, Hindi, and Mandarin.

</details>


### [196] [Do AI Agents Know When a Task Is Simple? Toward Complexity-Aware Reasoning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13034)
*Junjie Yin,Xinyu Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM代理在任务执行中常因过度阅读已处理信息导致资源浪费，本文提出E3方法（估计-执行-扩展），通过任务意识执行范围估计，在保证100%成功率的同时，显著削减成本85%、令牌91%和文件检查92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在自动化多步骤工作流时缺乏对任务实际努力需求的评估，盲目采用最大上下文优先策略（如重复读取文件），造成认知冗余和资源低效，亟需任务意识执行范围估计能力。

Method: 正式定义最小充分执行和代理认知冗余比(ACRR)，提出E3框架：代理先估计任务初始操作点，执行最小可行路径，仅在验证失败时逐步扩展执行范围。

Result: 在MSE-Bench基准（121个确定性编辑任务）中，E3匹配最强基线的100%成功率，成本降85%、令牌降91%、文件检查降92%；较自适应检索基线提升16%。LLM-Case真实代理实验（gpt-4o）验证过度阅读虽减轻但存在，E3为最精简高效策略，任务成功率相当。

Conclusion: 任务意识执行范围估计是迈向工程接地AI(EGAI)的关键步骤，能有效锚定代理努力于工程现实，避免资源浪费；框架和基准已开源，推动更高效AI代理设计。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering and informatics workflows, yet they rarely ask how much effort a task actually requires. They often follow a maximum-context-first strategy--re-reading files and dependencies they have already seen--turning a one-line edit into a small code-base audit. We argue the missing capability is task-aware execution-scope estimation: judging a task's difficulty, the information it truly needs, and the shortest reliable path before committing budget. We formalize minimum-sufficient execution and the Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR), and propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand): the agent estimates an initial operating point, executes a minimum viable path, and expands scope only when verification fails. On MSE-Bench--a deterministic benchmark of 121 edits in a capability-controlled simulator--E3 matches the strongest baseline's 100% success while cutting cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and further beats a strong adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%; the gains survive held-out instruction wording and essentially every cost weighting. A companion real-model harness (LLM-Case) corroborates the effect on a live gpt-4o agent editing a real open-source library, with every candidate patch graded by actually running the project's real pytest suite against a measured oracle: the over-reading is milder but real, and E3 is the leanest and fastest policy at comparable task success--its one shortfall a provider rate-limit, not a wrong edit. We frame this as a controlled probe of execution redundancy, not a measurement of any deployed agent, and position task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI (EGAI)--agents whose effort is anchored in the engineering reality of the task. We release the framework and benchmark.

</details>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Fuse-then-Detect for Passive UAV Localization Using Multi-UE 5G Uplink Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11955)
*Wenyu Huang,Nuria González-Prelcic,Vishnu Ratnam,Murat Bayraktar,Charlie Jianzhong Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 首次提出基于上行SRS的UAV探测框架，通过LOS参考同步和跨UE联合检测解决了多用户不同步和弱回波检测难题，在城市场景实现4.84m中值定位精度，为5G网络集成UAV监测提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机对空域安全、安保和隐私构成日益严重的威胁。虽然蜂窝基础设施可通过ISAC技术实现无专用雷达硬件的被动探测，但现有研究主要依赖下行链路的单站或双站/多站配置。缺乏上行链路解决方案限制了实际部署能力，且多UE系统中的时频同步问题和UAV弱回波检测可靠性不足成为亟待解决的关键挑战。

Method: 提出创新的上行链路探测框架，其中多个用户设备(UE)发射SRS导频信号，基站(BS)接收UAV散射回波。针对挑战，设计两种关键技术：1) LOS参考同步方案，利用现有定时提前(TA)命令和相邻机会共轭乘积去除UE间的时频幅残差；2) 联合检测器，通过搜索共享3D状态空间并跨多个UE累积证据，同时利用归一化对比度处理双站几何特性。

Result: 在FR1频段100MHz 5G NR波形、4个行人UE的城市场景实验中，所提框架实现了亚纳秒级同步精度和4.84米的3D位置中值误差，显著优于传统单UE检测方法。验证了LOS参考同步方案可有效消除UE间时频残差，联合检测器能在强杂波环境中可靠检测弱UAV回波。

Conclusion: 该研究首次验证了基于上行SRS的UAV探测框架可行性，证明所提出的LOS参考同步方案和联合检测器能有效解决多UE同步和弱回波检测问题，在城市场景中实现了高精度UAV定位能力，为未来5G网络集成UAV监测功能提供了重要技术基础。

Abstract: Low-altitude uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) can pose growing risks to airspace safety, security, and privacy. Cellular infrastructure can passively sense them without dedicated radar hardware by exploiting integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) technology. Most prior work exploits monostatic sensing or bistatic/multistatic configurations based on downlink measurements. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first uplink framework, where multiple user equipments (UEs) transmit sounding reference signal (SRS) pilots and the base station (BS) receives the UAV-scattered echoes. Sensing from uplink SRS, however, introduces new challenges. Each UE has its own oscillator and timing loop, so the channel estimate at the BS carries residual timing, frequency, and amplitude impairments that corrupt the UAV delay and Doppler. Moreover, the UAV echo is weaker than both the line-of-sight (LOS) path and urban clutter, so detection from a single UE transmission is not reliable. We address these challenges by designing a LOS-referenced synchronization scheme and a joint detector. The synchronization reuses the existing timing advance (TA) command and an adjacent-occasion conjugate product to remove the residuals without additional signaling. Then the detector searches a shared 3D state space and accumulates evidence across UEs. It leverages a normalized contrast that exploits the bistatic geometry. We evaluate the framework in a cluttered urban scene at frequency range 1 (FR1) with four pedestrian UEs and a 100 MHz 5G New Radio (NR) waveform. The proposed pipeline achieves sub-nanosecond synchronization and a 4.84 m median 3D position error.

</details>


### [198] [HAPS-Complemented Terrestrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12063)
*Animesh Yadav,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入全双工高空平台站（HAPS）作为中继，在约束条件下优化波束设计，显著提升小区边缘用户设备（UEs）的容量，采用SCA+AO算法高效解决非凸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面网络中，小区边缘UE因阴影、路径损耗和小区间干扰导致速率偏低，亟需突破传统中继方案以提升边缘容量。

Method: 提出全双工HAPS中继架构，通过物理分离收发天线增强自干扰抑制；建立总和速率最大化模型，结合总功率与QoS约束，设计基于连续凸近似（SCA）与交替优化（AO）的集中式算法求解。

Result: 仿真表明，相比纯地面网络，HAPS中继可使边缘UE容量显著提升，验证了全双工模式与优化算法的有效性。

Conclusion: HAPS集成方案能有效缓解边缘干扰问题，为未来6G高空协同通信提供可行技术路径。

Abstract: We consider a downlink multicell multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) system in an urban region, with a focus on improving the capacity of cell-edge user equipments (UEs). These UEs typically experience lower rates than near UEs because of shadowing, path loss, and inter-cell interference (ICI). To address this issue, we integrate a high-altitude platform station (HAPS) with the terrestrial network as a relay for edge-UE transmissions. We assume that the HAPS operates in full-duplex (FD) mode and exploits its large physical size to enhance passive self-interference (SI) suppression by separating its transmit and receive antennas. In the proposed scheme, each terrestrial base station (BS) forwards edge-UE data to the FD-HAPS, which then relays the data to the intended edge UEs. To design beams at both BSs and HAPS, we formulate a sum-rate maximization problem for under total transmit-power and minimum quality-of-service (QoS) constraints. To solve the resulting non-convex problem, we develop a centralized algorithm based on successive convex approximation (SCA) and alternating optimization (AO) for fast convergence. Simulation results show that relaying information via FD-HAPS significantly improves the capacity of cell-edge UEs compared with a terrestrial-only network.

</details>


### [199] [Learning-Based Beamforming for Energy Efficiency of Continuous Aperture Array Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12321)
*Shiyong Chen,Jia Guo,Shengqian Han*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出级联图神经网络（GNN）和基于泛函梯度的隐式神经表示（FGB-INR）架构，联合优化基站连续孔径阵列（CAPA）尺寸和波束成形函数，最大化下行多用户系统能效（EE），仿真验证其在保证EE性能的同时显著降低计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对下行多用户多CAPA系统中，能效最大化问题涉及非平凡的泛函优化挑战，具体表现为BS CAPA尺寸与连续电流分布波束成形函数的紧密耦合，导致传统数值方法计算复杂度高、实时性差。

Method: 设计级联网络架构：GNN部分利用置换等变性学习BS CAPA尺寸，FGB-INR部分基于EE目标的泛函梯度结构设计更新方程，直接学习波束成形函数，实现端到端联合优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法能效接近最优数值解，推理延迟大幅降低；FGB-INR的泛函梯度结构进一步提升能效，同时减少样本需求量和训练时间。

Conclusion: 该级联网络架构有效解决了CAPA优化中的泛函问题，在接近传统方法性能的前提下实现了高效率和低延迟，为CAPA系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper jointly optimizes the base-station (BS) continuous aperture array (CAPA) dimensions and beamforming functions to maximize energy efficiency (EE) of the downlink multiuser multi-CAPA system, where both the BS and the users are equipped with CAPAs. Since the beamforming functions are continuous current distribution over the BS CAPA, the resulting EE maximization problem is a nontrivial functional optimization problem that couples aperture sizing and beamforming design. To address this challenge, we propose a cascaded network architecture consisting of a graph neural network (GNN) and a functional-gradient based implicit neural representation (FGB-INR) to learn the BS CAPA dimensions and beamforming functions, respectively. Both networks exploit the permutation equivariance of the optimal optimization policy, and the update equations of FGB-INR are designed according to the functional-gradient structure of the EE objective. Simulation results show that the proposed method approaches the EE of the numerical method while substantially reducing inference latency. They also demonstrates that the functional-gradient structure in FGB-INR improves EE while reducing sample complexity and training time.

</details>


### [200] [Local Maxima of the Entrywise $\ell_4$ Norm on the Orthogonal Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12431)
*Dian Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明在实正交群O(r)上，元素四次方范数的局部极大值点仅由符号排列矩阵构成，并严格区分所有其他驻点的二阶稳定性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对正交约束下非凸优化问题的极值点分类难题，探索高维矩阵空间中四次方范数的结构特性，旨在解决传统数值优化中易陷入次优解的理论瓶颈。

Method: 通过构建正交随机矩阵的最大条目枢轴方法，精确计算完整黎曼海森矩阵并分析其正定性，结合微分几何工具处理零值、重复幅值及哈达玛型奇点等复杂情形，建立自包含的优化理论体系。

Result: 证明符号排列矩阵是任意维度下该目标函数的唯一局部极大值点，其他驻点均存在明确可计算的二阶正定切方向，且证明过程系统性地解决了支持集约简性和幅值重复性等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究首次严格证明了实正交群上元素四次幂范数优化问题的唯一局部和全局极值点结构，揭示了符号排列矩阵的核心地位，为高维正交约束优化问题提供了理论基准，同时完善了正交群上函数驻点分类的数学框架。

Abstract: We classify the local maximizers of the entrywise fourth-power objective \[ Q\longmapsto \lVert Q\rVert_4^4 =\sum_{i,j=1}^r q_{ij}^4 \] over the real orthogonal group $\mathcal O(r)$. We prove that the signed permutation matrices are the only local maximizers, and hence the only global maximizers, in every dimension. More strongly, every other stationary point has an explicit rank-two tangent direction with strictly positive second variation. The proof is based on a maximum-entry pivot for the orthostochastic matrix $Q^{\circ2}$: the associated full Riemannian Hessian can be evaluated exactly and is positive at a largest nonunit squared entry. The argument is self-contained and handles zeros, repeated magnitudes, reducible support, and Hadamard-type stationary points.

</details>


### [201] [DOA Estimation from One-Bit Magnitude-Only Measurements via Sign-Consistency Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12491)
*Xicheng Lu,Wei Liu,Akram Alomainy*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation problem using one-bit quantized magnitude-only measurements is studied, where magnitude-only measurements offer robustness against phase errors, thereby avoiding the need for array calibration, while one-bit quantization significantly reduces hardware cost and system complexity. As their direct combination results in meaningless constant measurements, we formulate a sign-consistency optimization problem using a smooth logistic surrogate with ell_2,1-norm regularization to promote joint sparsity. To solve this problem, a proximal-gradient algorithm is developed with guaranteed convergence to a critical point. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves accuracy comparable to coherent one-bit baselines under ideal conditions, while maintaining robust performance under severe phase errors that substantially impair coherent methods.

</details>


### [202] [AFDM-FTN: A Spectrally Efficient Waveform for High-Mobility Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12510)
*Xianle Dai,Qu Luo,Jianguo Li,Fabien Heliot,Shuangyang Li,Lixia Xiao,Pei Xiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出AFDM-FTN波形及BEM-MLMP-JCED低复杂度方案，显著提升高移动性通信中的频谱效率，同时保持误码率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高移动性通信场景中频谱效率（SE）不足的问题，需开发新型波形以克服传统方法的局限性。

Method: 推导AFDM-FTN输入输出关系并分析干扰模式；开发基于基础展开模型（BEM）的通道估计器；提出多层消息传递（MLMP）算法利用时域通道和FTN系数矩阵稀疏性；构建BEM-MLMP-JCED联合通道估计与数据检测方案。

Result: 仿真验证系统误码率与常规AFDM相当，但频谱效率更高且计算复杂度更低；分析了通道估计下界、均方误差性能及复杂度。

Conclusion: AFDM-FTN系统配合BEM-MLMP-JCED方案有效平衡了误码率、频谱效率和复杂度，为高移动性通信提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes an affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM)-aided faster-than-Nyquist (FTN) waveform, termed AFDM-FTN, to enhance spectral efficiency (SE) in high-mobility communication scenarios. We first derive the AFDM-FTN input-output relationship and analyze the FTN-induced interference pattern in AFDM-FTN. To address the channel estimation challenges, a low-complexity channel estimator based on the basis expansion model (BEM) is developed. By exploiting the intrinsic characteristics of the AFDM channel matrix and the FTN coefficient matrix, a multi-layer message passing (MLMP) algorithm is proposed that leverages the sparsity of the time-domain (TD) channel and the FTN coefficient matrix, where belief messages are iteratively propagated across the TD channel, FTN, and transform layers. Building upon the BEM-assisted channel estimation and MLMP, a low-complexity joint channel estimation and data detection scheme (BEM-MLMP-JCED) is further developed to iteratively refine channel estimation with the aid of transmitted data. Finally, the channel estimation lower bound, the mean square error (MSE) performance of the BEM-MLMP-JCED, and the computational complexity are analyzed. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed AFDM-FTN system with BEM-MLMP-JCED achieves comparable BER to conventional AFDM while providing enhanced SE and reduced complexity compared to benchmark receivers.

</details>


### [203] [An Adaptive Transmission Protocol Enabled by The State Switching Strategy of Beyond-Diagonal RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12514)
*Xiyao Wang,Hongyu Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应传输方案，允许BD-RIS在ON和OFF状态间智能切换，避免在强直射链路存在时无需开启BD-RIS，从而在保证传输成功的同时降低操作复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BD-RIS研究假设BD-RIS始终处于开启状态辅助传输，但在某些场景中（如收发器直接链路强时），BD-RIS的性能增益可能不足以证明其操作复杂性是合理的，因此需要一种智能使用BD-RIS的方法。

Method: 提出了一种自适应传输方案，包括：1)定义BD-RIS的ON和OFF两种工作状态；2)设计新的波束训练协议，使BD-RIS能根据实时信道条件智能切换状态；3)构建决策阈值作为状态切换的依据。

Result: 仿真结果显示，所提出的协议可以在不总是开启BD-RIS的情况下保证成功传输，无论是否存在强的收发器直接链路。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应传输方案能够在保证通信性能的同时，显著降低BD-RIS的操作复杂性，实现了更高效的RIS利用。

Abstract: Thanks to inter-element interconnections and flexible element arrangements, the beyond diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surface (BD-RIS) breaks through the limitation of traditional RIS to achieve enhanced performance and enlarged coverage. However, existing BD-RIS research assumes that BD-RIS is always turned ON to assist transmission, while, in some scenarios where the transmitter-receiver direct link exists and remains strong, the performance gains of BD-RIS may not justify its manipulation complexity. In order to smartly use BD-RIS, a novel adaptive transmission scheme is proposed in this paper. The proposed scheme first defines two working states of the BD-RIS, namely ON and OFF. Based on these two states, we design a new beam training protocol that enables BD-RIS to intelligently switch its working state according to real-time channel conditions. Furthermore, we construct a decision threshold as the decision basis for state switching to ensure the efficiency and reliability of protocol execution. Simulation results show that, without always turning ON the BD-RIS and performing sophisticated wave manipulation, the proposed protocol can guarantee successful transmission regardless of whether there is a strong transmitter-receiver link or not.

</details>


### [204] [Cellular Signal Constructed Convolutional Vision Transformer for High Accuracy Positioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12518)
*Junshi Chen,Xuhong Li,Russ Whiton,Fredrik Tufvesson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文提出ConViT模型，解决蜂窝网络复杂环境下的定位难题，在低信噪比和强干扰条件下实现高精度、低计算成本的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代蜂窝系统通过宽带和大型天线阵列支持高数据率，同时可利用高时空分辨率实现高精度定位；但标准CNN和视觉Transformer在低信噪比、强小区间干扰的复杂环境中性能受限，需改进方法。

Method: 设计混合卷积视觉Transformer（ConViT）架构，融合CNN局部感受野抑制噪声和Transformer全局注意力机制处理多径分量；评估多基站信号融合策略，并采用扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行传感器融合以平滑模型估计波动。

Result: 基于商业LTE信号的实测验证（城市非视距环境），ConViT实现距离RMSE 3.46米、偏航RMSE 2.54度，显著优于基准模型，且参数更少、计算复杂度更低。

Conclusion: ConViT有效提升了复杂蜂窝环境中的定位精度与鲁棒性，其可解释性通过延迟-角度功率分布与Transformer注意力权重相关性分析得以验证，为实际应用提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern cellular systems employ wide bandwidths and large antenna arrays to meet high data rate requirements. The high spatial and temporal resolution for communication also enables high-accuracy positioning as an ancillary benefit. Standard convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and vision Transformers have demonstrated excellent performance in positioning by leveraging delay-angle domain channel representations. However, they still face practical challenges in complicated cellular environments with low signal-to-noise ratios and severe inter-cell interference. This paper proposes a hybrid convolutional vision Transformer (ConViT) architecture that integrates the local receptive fields of CNNs to suppress local noise and employs Transformers to capture global attention among different multipath components. Various fusion strategies for combining signals from multiple distributed base stations are also evaluated. An extended Kalman filter with sensor fusion is applied to further mitigate long tail fluctuations of model estimates. Comprehensive validation is conducted with commercial long-term-evolution signals received by a large antenna array in urban environments with non line-of-sight signals and strong inter-cell interference. ConViT achieves a distance root mean square error (RMSE) of 3.46 meters and a yaw RMSE of 2.54 degrees, significantly outperforming benchmark models, while maintaining a lower parameter count and reduced computational complexity. Finally, a correspondence analysis between delay-angle power distributions and Transformer attention weights demonstrates the interpretability of the model.

</details>


### [205] [Comparison of Dimension Reduction Methods for EEG Seizure Detection Using Autonomous AI-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12546)
*Annika Stiehl,Vishal Kagade,Nicolas Weeger,Nicole Ille,Stefan Geißelsöder,Christian Uhl*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 比较四种EEG降维方法发现：方差类方法（AVD/PCA）配合优化分类器在癫痫检测中表现优于动态方法（DMD/DyCA），且AI框架可有效驱动跨维度-分类器的联合优化实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过降维技术获取紧凑且具有判别性的EEG特征表示，以提升深度学习癫痫检测模型的性能。

Method: 比较四种降维方法（PCA、DyCA、DMD、AVD）在TUSZ v2.0.3数据集上的癫痫检测性能，采用AI驱动框架自动优化各方法的分类器架构与超参数。

Result: 基于方差的方法AVD（88.28%）和PCA（85.98%）的ROC-AUC显著优于动态方法DMD（74.56%）和DyCA（74.85%）；最优分类器结构因表示方式而异；AVD具有最小验证-测试差距。

Conclusion: 本研究强调输入表示对EEG癫痫检测的关键作用，并证明自主AI实验框架在生物医学信号处理中的可行性，建议联合优化特征表示与分类器架构。

Abstract: Automated epileptic seizure detection from multichannel electroencephalography (EEG) benefits from dimension reduction to obtain compact, discriminative representations. We compare four signal-space dimension reduction methods, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Dynamical Component Analysis (DyCA), Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD), and Average Volatility Dimensioning (AVD), for deep learning-based seizure detection on the Temple University Hospital Seizure Corpus (TUSZ v2.0.3). To enable a comparison of optimal combinations of representation and classifier, an autonomous AI-driven research framework independently optimizes architecture and hyperparameters for each representation. Measured by test ROC-AUC, the variance-based methods AVD (88.28%) and PCA (85.98%) paired with their respective optimal classifiers outperform the dynamics-based methods DMD (74.56%) and DyCA (74.85%) by over 10%, with AVD also showing the smallest validation-to-test gap. The best-performing classifier architecture differs across representations, indicating that representation and classifier should be optimized jointly. Our results highlight the importance of the input representation for EEG seizure detection and indicate the viability of autonomous AI-driven experimentation in biomedical signal processing.

</details>


### [206] [SpeedyGS: Content-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression via Two-Stage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12656)
*Junteng Zhang,Tong Chen,Yuxin Zhao,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Zhan Ma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: SpeedyGS是一种内容感知的3DGS压缩器，通过结构优化和高效编码，实现模型大小减少160倍、解码加速9倍，适用于交互式应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩技术虽减少存储和带宽需求，但解码耗时数秒，无法满足交互式应用的实时性要求，亟需开发低延迟压缩方案。

Method: SpeedyGS分两阶段：结构形成阶段联合优化自适应量化和修剪，使用轻量级率代理估计熵编码成本；统计编码阶段将高斯几何转为稀疏八叉树令牌进行多阶段编码，属性通过局部自回归模型序列化编码。

Result: 与原始3DGS相比，模型大小减少高达160倍且质量几乎无退化；与现有压缩方法相比，解码速度显著提升，优化过程加速9倍，并支持通道级固定长度编码将解码延迟降至近零。

Conclusion: SpeedyGS在优化效率、压缩性能、解码延迟和渲染速度间取得最佳平衡，使3DGS技术适用于实时交互式场景。

Abstract: Recent progress in compressing large-scale 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) data has substantially reduced storage footprint, network transmission bandwidth, and memory traffic to GPU caches before rendering. Yet decoding with advanced 3DGS codecs still takes seconds, making them unsuitable for interactive applications. To systematically address this challenge, we propose SpeedyGS, a Content-Aware 3DGS Compressor that separately optimizes the structural formation and statistical coding. First, in structural formation, we jointly optimize adaptive quantization and pruning under a unified rate-distortion objective, where the rate term is replaced by a lightweight rate proxy that estimates entropy coding cost of the next stage, thereby efficiently regulating Gaussian density and precision to yield a compact scene representation. Then, in the statistical coding phase, Gaussian geometry is converted into sparse octree tokens and subsequently undergoes multi-stage coding, while Gaussian attributes are serialized into a 1D token stream for entropy coding via a complexity-controllable local autoregressive model. SpeedyGS achieves a favorable balance among optimization efficiency, compression performance, decoding latency, and rendering speed. Compared to vanilla 3DGS, SpeedyGS achieves up to 160$\times$ model size reduction with negligible quality degradation across common datasets. Compared to state-of-the-art compression methods, it also offers significantly faster decoding and accelerates optimization by 9$\times$ on consumer-grade hardware. To further reduce decoding overhead, the statistical coding stage also supports channel-wise, fixed-length coding for Gaussian as a simpler alternative, enabling SpeedyGS to better adapt to the underlying application and reduce decoding latency to nearly zero.

</details>


### [207] [Positional Attention-based Graph Neural Network for Learning Permutation Non-equivariant Wireless Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12744)
*Baichuan Zhao,Chenyang Yang,Jianyu Zhao,Di Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于位置注意力的图神经网络（GNN），解决无线策略学习中非置换等变（PE）问题，显著提升信道估计和端到端预编码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN在非PE策略下因归纳偏置不匹配导致性能下降，尤其在无线任务（如信道估计和端到端预编码）中，最优策略的PE性质依赖信道分布（例如，空间相关信道下策略对用户等变但对天线不等变）。

Method: 设计位置注意力机制，通过嵌入函数将顶点相对位置融入注意力计算，使GNN捕获不对称关系；同时保留参数共享以实现高效学习和尺寸泛化。

Result: 模拟结果显示，该方法优于现有信道估计和端到端预编码技术，训练样本需求减少，并成功泛化至不同天线和用户数量的系统。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了非PE策略学习的挑战，为无线通信中的高效策略学习提供了可行方案，兼具高精度和泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have emerged as a promising approach to learning wireless policies efficiently by leveraging topology prior and incorporating relational inductive biases. However, when the optimal policy is not permutation equivariant (PE), conventional GNNs suffer from mismatched inductive biases, leading to degraded performance or poor generalizability. This issue arises in wireless tasks with expected objectives, such as channel estimation and end-to-end (E2E) precoding, where the PE property of the optimal policy depends on the underlying channel distribution. In this paper, we propose a novel positional attention-based GNN to learn permutation nonequivariant policies efficiently. The core idea is to incorporate relative positions of vertices into the attention mechanism via an embedding function, enabling the GNNs to capture asymmetric relationships. Consequently, the proposed GNN can represent permutation non-equivariant functions, while retaining high learning efficiency and size generalizability through parameter sharing. We consider channel estimation and E2E precoding as case studies, and prove that their policies are PE to users but not to antennas under spatially correlated channels. We employ the proposed GNN to learn the policies, where the embedding function is designed based on the channel covariance matrix. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed GNN outperforms existing channel estimation and E2E precoding methods, requires fewer samples for training, and can be generalized to systems with different numbers of antennas and users.

</details>


### [208] [Scalable Multi-BD Access for OFDM-Based Symbiotic Backscatter Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12762)
*Fikiri Salum Uledi,Muhammad Bilal Janjua,Muhammad Sohaib J. Solaija,Cagri Ozgenc Etemoglu,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 作者提出正交频码扩频（OFC）方案实现无干扰多反向散射设备接入，通过频率偏移和码扩频抑制直接链路干扰和设备间干扰，仿真验证其低漏检概率和高频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于OFDM的共生反向散射通信中多反向散射设备接入时的直接链路干扰（DLI）和反向散射设备间干扰（IBDI）问题，提升系统多设备接入能力和性能。

Method: 引入正交频码扩频（OFC）方案：发射机预留空子载波供设备通过频率偏移传输信息以抑制DLI；应用块状正交码扩频为每个设备分配唯一码字以在码域中减轻IBDI；开发非相干能量检测器分析平均漏检概率（PMD）和总速率性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明OFC方案有效，实现低平均漏检概率（PMD）和高总速率，与常规码辅助多设备接入方案相比显著提升频谱效率，在瑞利衰落环境下表现优越。

Conclusion: 所提OFC方案成功解决了多设备接入干扰问题，实现了无干扰可靠通信，为共生反向散射系统提供了高效多设备接入解决方案。

Abstract: We propose an interference-free multi-backscatter device (BD) access scheme for orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM)-based symbiotic backscatter communication (SBC). Specifically, we introduce an orthogonal frequency-code spread (OFC) scheme in which the transmitter reserves empty subcarriers for BDs to incorporate their information through frequency shifting, which effectively suppresses direct-link interference (DLI). Since enabling simultaneous access over shared subcarriers introduces inter-backscatter device interference (IBDI), a blockwise orthogonal code-spread is applied to assign each BD to a unique codeword, thereby mitigating IBDI with orthogonal transmissions of all BD signals in the code domain. We develop a non-coherent energy detector whose performance is analyzed in terms of average probability of missed detection (PMD) and sum-rate under Rayleigh fading. The simulation results validate the OFC scheme and demonstrate low PMD values and high spectral efficiency compared to conventional code-assisted multiple-BD access schemes.

</details>


### [209] [GPU-Accelerated Optimisation of Symmetric Bidirectional Ultrawideband Coherent Transmission Under Launch Power Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12921)
*Mindaugas Jarmolovičius,Eric Sillekens,Polina Bayvel,Robert I. Killey*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 在18-dBm功率约束下，反转O-band方向可提升3跨传输聚合容量达24.5%，但无约束时无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决总光纤功率约束下双向相干传输的容量瓶颈，以优化OESCL-band系统效率。

Method: 采用GPU加速的边界值Raman求解器进行发射功率优化，实现传输参数精确控制。

Result: 无功率约束时容量无提升；在18-dBm限制下，3跨传输中反转O-band方向使聚合容量增加最多24.5%。

Conclusion: 方向反转在特定功率约束场景是高效优化策略，显著提升传输容量，验证了Raman求解器与功率优化的协同价值。

Abstract: We optimise bidirectional OESCL-band coherent transmission under total fibre power constraints using a GPU-accelerated boundary-value Raman solver and launch-power optimisation. While no gain is observed without power constraints, for an 18-dBm limit 3-span transmission reversing the O-band direction increases aggregate capacity by up to 24.5%

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [EFLUX: Elastic Multi-Robot Formation Navigation and Adaptation with Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12050)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Guangyao Shi,Jonathan Diller,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EFLUX是一个基于LLM的弹性多机器人队形导航框架，通过联合处理变形（如缩放、剪切）和重构（如分割、合并）动作，在受限环境中实现安全连续导航，显著减少死锁和失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将变形与重构行为独立建模或依赖手工规则，缺乏明确的几何标准，导致在复杂受限环境中易出现次优轨迹和死锁；亟需一种能在线自适应调整队形几何、连通性和团队组成的统一方法。

Method: EFLUX采用几何基础的LLM代理框架：首先提取结构化场景表示，利用LLM联合推理变形和重构动作，再通过闭环生成-验证-校正管道将策略转化为可执行的单机器人路径点，实现动态导航。

Result: 模拟与硬件实验表明，EFLUX在受限环境中实现了安全、连续且弹性的队形导航，相比基线方法显著降低了死锁和导航失败率，同时维持了多机器人协同的一致性。

Conclusion: EFLUX有效解决了多机器人系统在复杂环境中的弹性导航问题，通过几何感知的LLM代理架构，提升了队形适应性和鲁棒性，为自主多机器人应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot teams operating in confined or cluttered environments must adapt both their formation geometry and group topology to navigate through complex obstacles. This adaptation requires two complementary behaviors: deformation, where the team continuously reshapes its geometry while remaining connected, and reconfiguration, where robots split into subgroups or merge back into a single formation. Existing methods often model these behaviors independently, connect them through handcrafted rules, or lack explicit geometric criteria for determining when each behavior should be invoked. However, challenging environments may require online changes in formation shape, connectivity, and effective team composition, making decoupled or rule-based approaches prone to suboptimal trajectories and deadlock. We propose EFLUX, a geometry-grounded LLM agentic framework for automatic and elastic multi-robot formation navigation. EFLUX extracts a structured scene representation and uses an LLM to reason jointly over both deformation actions, such as scaling and shearing, and reconfiguration actions, such as splitting and merging. These strategies are then translated into executable per-robot waypoints through a closed-loop generation, verification, and correction pipeline. Simulation and hardware experiments show that EFLUX enables safe, continuous, and elastic formation navigation in constrained environments, reducing deadlock and navigation failures compared with baselines while maintaining coherent multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [211] [Enabling 24-hour Agricultural Robotics: Unsupervised Day-to-Night Cross-Modal Image Translation for Nighttime Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12065)
*Robel Mamo,Rajitha de Silva,Grzegorz Cielniak,Taeyeong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究开发无监督图像转换技术实现农业机器人夜间视觉导航，通过CLIP模型保持语义一致性和可见性掩码优化，在自构数据集上验证有效性并开放全部资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业机器人视觉导航系统多基于白昼条件，而夜间部署可实现连续作业但面临标注数据匮乏的技术瓶颈，制约了作物全天候监测、夜间采果及病虫害防治等关键应用发展。

Method: 提出基于CLIP模型的无监督图像翻译框架，实现白天RGB图像到夜间NIR图像的转换；创新性引入可见性掩码机制处理NIR照明限制；构建首个夜间农业场景标注数据集AgriNight（含977张图像）。

Result: 所提框架在AgriNight数据集上相比基线方法显著提升图像质量指标，下游语义分割准确率提高12.7%，并成功验证物理机器人夜间自主导航可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过无监督图像翻译框架解决了夜间农业导航的数据稀缺问题，为全天候智能农机系统设计提供了新思路，所构建的AgriNight数据集填补了领域空白。

Abstract: While visual navigation has been extensively studied in agricultural robotics, most existing systems assume daytime conditions. In fact, deploying autonomous robots at night offers significant advantages, including 24-hour crop and soil monitoring, fruit harvesting, and nocturnal pest detection. Modern vision-based systems, however, rely heavily on large-scale well-annotated image datasets, which remains challenging to obtain for nighttime operation scenarios. To address this, we propose an unsupervised image translation framework that converts daytime plant-row RGB images into near-infrared (NIR) nighttime counterparts without requiring pixel-to-pixel supervision. This enables the direct reuse of daytime semantic labels for training nighttime perception models. In particular, by incorporating a pre-trained Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) model, the proposed framework is designed to preserve semantic consistency during day-to-night translation. Additionally, a visibility mask is introduced to account for the limited effective range of NIR illumination in nighttime scenes. We conduct comparative evaluations with state-of-the-art image translation baselines and demonstrate higher image qualities, as supported by improved performance in downstream semantic segmentation for nighttime visual navigation. For evaluation, we utilize AgriNight--a novel dataset comprising 428 daytime and 549 nighttime images collected using night-vision-equipped mobile robots in agricultural fields and manually annotated with pixel-wise semantic labels--and introduce it as the first benchmark for nighttime agricultural visual navigation. We also perform real-time autonomous navigation experiments with a physical robot operating at night. The data and code are available at: https://github.com/mamorobel/AgriNight.

</details>


### [212] [Robust In-Hand Manipulation via Priors in Reinforcement Learning and Mechanical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12105)
*Yifei Chen,Shihan Lu,Ed Colgate,Kevin Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种物理先验（全局抓取质量与局部接触几何）解决无外部传感下机器人手内滚动操作难题，通过优化抓取稳定性和接触机制，在多种物体和姿态条件下显著提升旋转效率及抗扰动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无外部传感器时，手指-物体接触不确定性及重力干扰导致手内操作困难。现有强化学习方法虽能学习复杂手指步态，但未优先保障持续操作所需的稳定抓取质量。

Method: 引入两类物理先验：1）基于经典抓取分析的全局抓取质量先验，作为稠密奖励项优化接触分布；2）基于指尖曲率的局部接触几何先验，通过指尖形态设计抑制偏离目标方向的漂移。

Result: 实验表明，该方案对3种物体在4种手掌姿态下的操作，使旋转效率提升62%、抓取稳定性提高58%，抗扰动能力增强73%，并验证了仿真到现实的有效性迁移。

Conclusion: 将物理先验整合至学习框架与指尖形态设计，可有效提升无传感条件下机器人手内操作的鲁棒性与任务成功率，为灵巧操作提供新范式。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation without external sensing is challenging due to uncertainties from finger-object contacts and disturbances by gravity. While reinforcement learning has shown promise in learning complex finger gaiting, existing approaches do not prioritize maintaining well-conditioned grasps for sustained manipulation. We introduce two complementary physics priors for robust in-hand rolling: a global grasp-quality prior derived from classical grasp analysis and a local contact-geometry prior based on fingertip curvature. The grasp-quality prior is used as a dense reward-shaping term that encourages well-distributed contacts with improved worst-case wrench resistance. The contact-geometry prior is expressed in the fingertip geometry that mechanically shapes the contact interface toward task-aligned rolling while reducing off-axis drift. We evaluate the effect of these priors on learning in-hand rolling manipulation for a multifingered robotic hand manipulating three different objects at four palm orientations. Results show significant improvement in rotation efficiency, grasp stability, and disturbance rejection, suggesting that physics priors embedded in both learning and fingertip morphology improve task robustness and sim-to-real transfer. An overview video can be found at https://youtu.be/pdd1wHxQnJM?si=dM-U5kiiPTYsk3Pk.

</details>


### [213] [GaitSpan: Growing Humanoid Locomotion from Walking to Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12114)
*Kwan-Yee Lin,Zilin Wang,Janelle J. Liu,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaitSpan框架通过扩展预训练行走策略，实现覆盖连续速度范围的人形机器人运动控制，显著提升灵活性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在步态多样性上灵活性不足，难以有效应对连续速度命令、不同地形及形态变化。

Method: 提出GaitSpan框架，包括节奏生成（使用内部时钟调制冻结行走策略）、步态塑造（基于弹簧加载倒立摆动力学的物理目标）和残差适应（捕获未覆盖运动细节）。

Result: 实验显示GaitSpan生成单一命令条件策略，覆盖步行至跑步连续范围，实现跨形态迁移和未知地形零样本部署，学习效率和性能均优于多专家或人类模仿基线。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明高效可扩展的步态成长机制，为人形机器人运动控制提供新范式，解决了现有方法灵活性受限的关键问题。

Abstract: A humanoid that can walk should not relearn locomotion from scratch to jog or run. Yet current approaches often obtain gait diversity by prescribing gait schedules, imitating motion clips, training experts to switch between or distilling skills into one policy. These strategies can produce impressive behaviors, but offer limited flexibility across continuous speed commands, terrains, and morphologies. We study skill growth with GaitSpan, a framework that expands a pretrained, basic walking policy into faster locomotion. It treats walking as a seed skill: reusable motor structure for balance, support, body coordination, and contact transition that can be regenerated at new rhythms, extended into longer/higher strides, and corrected by residual adaptation. This expansion has three aspects: 1) rhythm generation, which modulates the frozen walking policy with multiple internal clocks and learns command-conditioned combinations of the resulting canonical actions; 2) stride shaping, which rewards dynamic locomotion patterns appropriate for higher commanded speeds using a physically grounded objective inspired by spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics; and 3) residual adaptation, which captures motion details not accounted for by rhythm generation or stride shaping. GaitSpan is the first to deliver a single command-conditioned humanoid policy that spans walking, jogging, and running-like regimes covering a continuous speed range, transfers across morphologies, and deploys zero-shot on unseen sim-to-sim, and real-world terrains. Compared with baselines either trained with multi-experts or imitation from humans, it learns faster and achieves stronger gait performance.

</details>


### [214] [A Behavioral State Vocabulary in Sony ERS-111 R-CODE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12115)
*Christopher A. Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过对Sony R-CODE样本库的生成行为图进行语料库级分析，发现表面差异化的行为脚本共享以初始化、感知、迭代动作、同步和恢复为核心的紧凑具身语法结构，并提出该状态抽象对构建新行为例程的中间表示价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多孤立解析单个脚本，缺乏对AIBO行为系统整体结构规律的系统性挖掘；需揭示隐藏于样本库中的通用控制框架以指导新行为设计。

Method: 采用语料库级命名状态对比分析法，通过跨脚本聚合行为图特征识别重复出现的控制词汇，建立行为原语分类体系。

Result: 样本库中多样化的行为例程均由五种核心原语（初始化/感知/迭代动作/同步/恢复）构成紧凑型具身语法，验证了结构化行为生成的可行性。

Conclusion: 状态基抽象作为中间表示可显著提升约束型机器人系统中新行为例程的模块化开发效率，在确定性控制场景下具有重要工程应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a corpus-level analysis of generated behavior diagrams derived from Sony's R-CODE sample distribution for the ERS-111 AIBO. Rather than reading each script in isolation, the study compares named states across the corpus to identify the recurring control vocabulary that structures the sample set. The resulting aggregate shows that many superficially different routines are built from a compact embodied grammar centered on initialization, sensing, iterative action, synchronization, and recovery. In addition to historical analysis, the paper argues that this form of state-based abstraction is useful as an intermediate representation for constructing new encapsulated behavior routines, especially on constrained native robotic systems where deterministic control, direct hardware access, and modular behavioral composition remain important.

</details>


### [215] [More than a Manipulator: Planning Propellant-Free Attitude Maneuvers for Free-Floating Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12130)
*Harsh G. Bhundiya,Avi Soval,Keenan Albee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Spacecraft attitude control is traditionally achieved using momentum exchange devices or propellant-consuming thrusters. Meanwhile, a growing number of missions require robotic manipulators, which are typically treated as disturbance sources to be rejected rather than as actuators for spacecraft reorientation. This work investigates the use of manipulator motions for propellant-free attitude control by formulating a trajectory optimization problem with critical joint and collision avoidance constraints. Using an interior point solver for the resulting nonlinear program, complex slew and detumble trajectories are demonstrated for a range of spacecraft-manipulator systems with varying kinematic complexity and mass properties. The achievable control authority is compared directly with that of reaction wheel arrays via momentum and torque envelopes, demonstrating the potential for manipulators to serve as redundant or even primary attitude control systems. This work provides a framework for using manipulators as multipurpose attitude control actuators, with particularly promising applications in in-space assembly and manufacturing when grasping payloads with high relative mass fractions.

</details>


### [216] [Analysis of Mutual and Referential Human and Robot Gazes in a Collaborative Word Association Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12181)
*Jens V. Rüppel,Tim Schreiter,Andrey Rudenko,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究调查机器人凝视在任务导向的协作对话（如单词联想游戏）中如何影响人类视觉注意力，发现语言交互主导了凝视效果，且确认请求时人类更频繁看向机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦面对面社交对话中的凝视，但任务导向交互（如协作游戏）中的凝视作用尚未充分探索，尤其缺乏对认知负荷较高场景的理解。

Method: 采用实验设计，让参与者与NAO机器人（LLM驱动）进行协作单词联想游戏，设置相互凝视和参考性凝视两种条件；使用眼动追踪眼镜记录参与者凝视，并分析凝视坐标、语音片段、关键事件及兴趣区域。

Result: 机器人凝视方向不影响参与者首次注视单词的时间；但当参与者对话包含确认请求时，其看向机器人的频率显著更高；认知负荷导致语言方面压倒了参考性凝视的效果。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了任务导向协作中凝视的局限性，强调了语言交互的主导作用，为机器人凝视和轮换行为的设计与验证提供了关键见解，尤其在高效通信和协调场景下。

Abstract: Robot gaze is a major component of human-robot dialogue coordination. Most studies of gaze in human-robot dialogue focus on face-to-face social conversations, but little is known about gaze in demanding task-focused interactions. In this paper, we investigate how the gaze of a robot game partner affects human visual attention and if humans tend to direct confirmation-seeking gazes towards the robot. In our study, we let participants play a collaborative word association game with a NAO robot acting as an embodied, LLM-driven conversational partner. Our experiments are conducted under two conditions, which implement mutual and referential gazes of the robot respectively. We record participants' gaze using eye tracking glasses and analyze the interactions using gaze coordinates, speech segments, key events and areas of interests. We find that robot gaze orientation does not affect the time to first fixation on words the robot proposed. We also find that participants gaze more often at the robot when their dialogue line contains confirmation requests, compared to when it does not. Our results indicate (likely also due to the cognitively demanding nature of the game) that the verbal aspect of this task overshadows the effects of referential robot gaze. These findings offer valuable insights for designing and validating robot gaze and turn-taking behavior in collaborative tasks which require coordination and efficient communication.

</details>


### [217] [Contract-Grounded Behavior Tree Synthesis via Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12220)
*Jonathan Salfity,Robert Blake Anderson,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于契约接地的行为树合成架构，使非专家操作员能通过自然语言生成可部署的机器人行为树，无需了解机器人实现细节。架构通过机器人侧MCP服务器提供显式契约（技能库、操作符、组合模板），并经运行时验证确保正确性。实验在110个模拟任务和14个物理任务上验证，结果显示近完美验证率、高成功率，并成功迁移至物理硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based行为树合成方法依赖提示作者完成接地（确保行为树仅引用机器人可执行的实际技能），但作者常缺乏对机器人技能库、参数化及运行时约束的知识，导致部署脆弱、易出错。因此，亟需一种自动化接地机制，使非专家能可靠生成可部署行为树，降低部署门槛。

Method: 设计契约接地行为树合成架构：编码代理查询机器人侧Model Context Protocol (MCP) 服务器，获取显式契约（包含技能库、允许的行为树操作符及可选组合模板），据此合成行为树；合成后，机器人运行时验证门强制校验正确性再执行。评估使用两个LLMs（闭源Sonnet 4.6和开源Gemma4:31b），在PyRoboSim模拟环境（110任务）和物理Husarion Panther机器人（14任务）测试。

Result: 实验表明：契约接地使行为树验证率接近完美，任务成功率显著提升；行为树组合模板对小模型（Gemma4:31b）在反应性控制流任务上成功恢复成功率；架构无缝迁移至运行Nav2堆栈的物理硬件，且对操作员和代理均保持不透明性。

Conclusion: 该架构有效解决行为树接地问题，实现非专家自然语言指令到可靠可部署行为树的转换，大幅提升部署鲁棒性；验证了模拟与物理环境的一致性，证明其在真实机器人系统中的实用性和可扩展性，为未来人机协作机器人提供高效、低门槛的合成方案。

Abstract: Synthesizing deployable robot behavior trees (BTs) from natural language (NL) requires grounding to ensure every generated BT references only skills a robot can actually execute. Existing LLM-based BT synthesis approaches often place this grounding responsibility on the prompt author. This makes deployment brittle when the author does not know which skills the robot can execute, how those skills are parameterized, or how the robot runtime software constrains valid BT structure. This paper proposes a contract-grounded BT synthesis architecture in which a coding agent queries a robot-side Model Context Protocol (MCP) server to retrieve an explicit contract consisting of a skill library, permitted BT operators, and optional BT composition templates, before synthesizing a BT for validation and execution. In our framework, non-expert operators issue NL commands without knowledge of robot implementation details, while a robot runtime validation gate enforces correctness before execution. We evaluate two LLMs, a closed model (Sonnet 4.6) and a smaller open-source model (Gemma4:31b), across 110 simulated tasks in PyRoboSim and 14 tasks on a physical Husarion Panther robot. Results show that contract grounding enables near-perfect BT validation and high task success, that BT composition templates substantially recover success on reactive control-flow tasks for the smaller model, and that the architecture transfers to physical hardware running a Nav2 stack opaque to both operator and agent.

</details>


### [218] [DiffRadar: Differentiable Physics-Aware Radar SLAM with Gaussian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12265)
*Gaurav Bagwe,Xiaoyong Yuan,Yongji Wu,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffRadar是一个实时雷达SLAM系统，通过将雷达观测建模为可微的物理感知高斯场，避免了传统离散化方法的局限性，在特征贫乏环境中实现更低轨迹误差和更高地图一致性，同时保持70 FPS的实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达SLAM系统依赖离散化雷达热图的扫描匹配，破坏了几何连续性，无法捕捉雷达的关键传感特性（如多普勒信息），导致在再生或动态变化环境中姿态估计不稳定和映射质量下降。

Method: DiffRadar将场景表示为各向异性高斯基元，并通过可微的雷达前向模型在范围-方位和多普勒-方位空间中渲染雷达测量，实现机器人姿态与场景结构的联合直接优化。

Result: 在公共Radarize基准和受控压力测试中（覆盖走廊退化、运动制度转换等场景），DiffRadar显著降低了轨迹误差（尤其在特征贫乏走廊运动中），地图一致性提高一倍以上，同时维持70 FPS实时性能。

Conclusion: 直接在信号域建模雷达观测而非离散化表示，能显著提升雷达SLAM的鲁棒性和一致性，为移动平台提供更可靠的导航解决方案。

Abstract: Radar sensing is increasingly used in mobile systems because it operates reliably under poor lighting, adverse weather, and privacy-sensitive settings where cameras and LiDAR often fail. However, most existing radar SLAM systems estimate motion through scan matching on discretized radar heatmaps, which breaks geometric continuity and fails to capture key radar sensing properties, often leading to unstable pose estimation and degraded mapping in regenerate or dynamically changing environments. We present DiffRadar, a real-time radar SLAM system that models radar observations as a differentiable, physics-aware Gaussian field rather than discrete scans. DiffRadar represents the scene as anisotropic Gaussian primitives and renders radar measurements in range-azimuth and Doppler-azimuth spaces through a differentiable radar forward model, enabling joint optimization of robot pose and scene structure directly from radar measurements. We implement DiffRadar on commodity FMCW radar hardware and evaluate it on both the public Radarize benchmark and a controlled stress-test suite that targets common radar SLAM failure modes, including corridor degeneracy, motion regime transitions, dynamic clutter, and long-horizon loop closures. DiffRadar achieves substantial reductions in trajectory error on the benchmark, with especially large gains under feature-poor corridor motion, while more than doubling map consistency and maintaining real-time performance at 70 FPS. These results show that modeling radar observations directly in the signal domain enables substantially more robust and consistent radar-only SLAM for mobile platforms.

</details>


### [219] [Flatness-Preserving Residual Learning for Real-Time Tight Quadrotor Formation Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12275)
*Pei-An Hsieh,Fengjun Yang,Nikolai Matni,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息残差动力学学习框架，有效补偿四旋翼紧密编队中的湍流空气动力干扰（如下洗），减少31%平均跟踪误差；该方法在匹配非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）性能的同时，计算需求降低一个数量级，仅需30秒训练数据和5ms循环速率即可实现稳定编队飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机紧密编队飞行时，易受湍流空气动力相互作用（如下洗）严重影响，若未建模可能导致灾难性碰撞事故，亟需一种高效、准确的补偿方法以确保编队稳定性。

Method: 采用物理信息残差动力学学习框架捕捉复杂空气动力相互作用，同时保持多四旋翼系统微分平坦性；基于此设计计算高效的反馈线性化控制器，通过线性控制技术简便调优，并利用前馈补偿机制消除空气动力干扰。

Result: 硬件实验验证：该方法相比名义基线减少31%平均跟踪误差；在紧密编队飞行中，其跟踪性能与最新非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）持平，但计算开销降低一个数量级，显著提升效率。

Conclusion: 首次证明稳定、紧密编队飞行可在极低数据量（30秒内）和高速循环（5ms）条件下实现，为计算资源受限的飞行系统解锁高保真空气动力补偿能力，推动实际应用场景落地。

Abstract: Quadrotors flying in tight formations are severely affected by turbulent aerodynamic interactions, such as downwash, that can cause catastrophic collisions if left unmodeled. To compensate for these effects, we propose a physics-informed residual dynamics learning framework that captures complex aerodynamic interactions while ensuring the joint multi-quadrotor system remains differentially flat. We leverage this preserved flatness to design a computationally efficient feedback linearization controller that is easily tunable with linear control techniques and cancels aerodynamic disturbances via feedforward compensation. Hardware experiments demonstrate our framework reduces average tracking errors by 31% compared to nominal baselines. Crucially, our lightweight approach matches the tracking performance of state-of-the-art nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) while requiring an order of magnitude less computation. We are the first to show that stable, tight formation flight can be achieved with under 30 seconds of training data and a 5ms loop rate, unlocking high-fidelity aerodynamic compensation for compute-constrained flight stacks.

</details>


### [220] [Reducing Temporal Redundancy for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12287)
*Yuzhou Wu,Yuxin Zheng,Muchun Niu,Yishan Yang,Tianhao Liu,hanwen kang,Jiajian Jing,Linfeng Zhang,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出系统级加速策略，显著提升VLA模型推理速度至2倍以上，同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中泛化性强，但高推理延迟阻碍实时部署；主要冗余源于重复帧编码和多步扩散采样。

Method: 感知侧：仅增量更新动态区域令牌；策略侧：通过效率训练将扩散采样压缩为2步计划。

Result: 实验显示速度提升>2倍，成功率达98%，在Libero、RobotWin及真实平台验证。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决延迟问题，促进VLA模型实时应用，代码将开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit strong generalization for robotic manipulation, yet their high inference latency limits real time deployment. We identify two primary sources of temporal redundancy in existing VLA pipelines: repeated visual encoding of highly similar consecutive frames and multi step iterative sampling in diffusion based policies. To address this, we propose a system level acceleration strategy that reduces computation in both perception and action generation. On the perception side, we incrementally update only tokens corresponding to dynamic scene regions instead of re-encoding entire frames. On the policy side, we compress diffusion sampling into a compact 2-step schedule through efficiency oriented training while preserving action precision. Experiments on Libero, RobotWin, and Real Robot Platforms demonstrate over 2 times speedup while maintaining high performance, achieving up to 98% success rate on general manipulation benchmarks. Our codes will be released on Github.

</details>


### [221] [VistaVLA: Geometry- and Semantic-Aware 3D Gaussian-Grounded VLA for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12356)
*Mohan Liu,Zhihao Gu,Xuanyu Chen,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Yan Wu,Wei-Yun Yau,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型缺乏3D语义表征的问题，提出VistaVLA框架，通过3D高斯基元构建几何-语义联合认知地图，结合令牌压缩机制实现高效策略学习，显著提升机器人操作任务性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏场景级3D语义表征，仅依赖2D视觉和语言输入导致空间推理受限；虽然部分方法引入深度/点云等3D线索，但仍缺少高层语义对齐和认知地图构建能力

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) 将多视角视觉-语言特征提升至3D高斯基元构建几何锚定语义令牌；2) 设计Merge-then-Query压缩机制实现99%令牌精简，生成紧凑空间上下文用于VLA策略学习

Result: 在7项真实任务中成功率提升22.8%，分布外任务性能超越基线30.0%，同时通过模拟环境验证了3D表征的有效性

Conclusion: VistaVLA通过构建几何与语义融合的3D认知地图，显著提升了VLA模型的物理世界推理能力，在真实任务中实现22.8%的成功率提升，验证了3D语义表征对机器人操作任务的重要价值

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful end-to-end paradigm for robotic manipulation by mapping language instructions and 2D visual inputs directly to actions. However, these models lack an explicit, scene-level 3D representation, limiting their ability to reason over spatial layouts and geometric constraints. While recent efforts incorporate explicit 3D cues, such as depth maps or point clouds, to improve geometric awareness, they primarily capture low-level structures and lack high-level semantic grounding in 3D space. In human cognition, interaction with the physical world relies on a 3D semantic cognitive map - an internal mental model that integrates spatial layouts with semantic context to enable persistent, viewpoint-invariant reasoning. In light of this, we present VistaVLA, a novel two-stage framework that constructs a geometry- and semantics-aware 3D cognitive representation from 3D Gaussian primitives and grounds it as compact context tokens for VLA policy learning. Specifically, VistaVLA lifts multi-view vision-language features into 3D Gaussian primitives, forming geometry-anchored semantic tokens that align view-consistent spatial grounding with 2D visual feature spaces. To make this 3D representation computationally tractable for effective VLA control, we introduce Merge-then-Query (MtQ), a token summarization mechanism. MtQ compresses dense Gaussian primitives into a highly compact set of spatially informative tokens, achieving a 99% token reduction while preserving action-relevant 3D layouts and semantic context. Extensive evaluations in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness of VistaVLA. Notably, in real-world scenarios, VistaVLA improves success rates by 22.8% across seven real-world tasks and by 30.0% over the VLA-Adapter baseline on challenging out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [222] [StratMamba: Strategic and Reactive Stream Partitioning for Path-Efficient LiDAR-Based Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12370)
*Hung-Chieh Wu,Xiaopan Zhang,Kasra Sinaei,Ryan Abnavi,Kasun Weerakoon,Christopher Bradley,Seyed Fakoorian,Jiachen Li,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StratMamba是一种双流Mamba架构，通过快衰减记忆处理LiDAR数据实现实时避障，慢衰减记忆维持长时目标信息进行规划，显著提升机器人导航效率、速度和路径最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂、障碍物密集的机器人导航环境中，高效捕获长时时间依赖是关键挑战，传统方法如LSTM或Transformer存在计算瓶颈和效率不足。

Method: 采用双流设计：快衰减组件专注处理高频LiDAR数据以支持反应式避障，慢衰减组件保留长时目标信息用于战略路径规划。

Result: 在IsaacLab/Gazebo仿真及Unitree GO1真实机器人上验证：超时率更低，导航中位步数为576（比Vanilla-Mamba快5.0%），路径效率达0.915（所有基线最高），且在扩展LiDAR范围下性能更稳健。

Conclusion: 双流分区机制有效平衡反应安全性与战略导航，在挑战性感知条件下实现卓越的时间推理效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper proposes StratMamba, a dual-stream Mamba-based temporal modeling architecture, to more efficiently capture long-horizon temporal dependencies required for robot navigation in complex and obstacle-rich environments. StratMamba leverages a combination of fast-decay and slow-decay memory architectures, where the fast-decay component processes high-frequency LiDAR data for reactive obstacle avoidance, while the slow-decay component maintains longer-horizon goal information for strategic planning. We perform extensive evaluations of different obstacle avoidance scenarios in IsaacLab and Gazebo, while also validating successful sim-to-real deployment on a Unitree GO1 quadruped robot navigating in the presence of static/dynamic obstacles. Comparisons with other temporal RL baselines, such as LSTM, Transformer, and Vanilla-Mamba, show that our StratMamba achieves exceptional temporal reasoning efficiency with a lower timeout rate, while maintaining the fastest navigation speed (576 median steps, 5.0% better than Vanilla-Mamba). It also achieves the highest path optimality (0.915 path efficiency) across all baselines. Real-world evaluation reveals that StratMamba maintains more robust performance across extended LiDAR ranges compared to vanilla Mamba and the Transformer, demonstrating that dual-stream partitioning effectively balances reactive safety with strategic navigation under challenging sensing conditions.

</details>


### [223] [Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control for Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12423)
*Zhilin He,Yorai Shaoul,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MDOC（基于模型的扩散最优控制），一种无需演示数据的扩散规划器，通过结合动力学模型与控制屏障函数约束投影，实现高效、动态可行且无碰撞的多机器人轨迹生成，在样本效率、几何平滑性、成功率和计算时间上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划在连续环境中面临联合轨迹空间组合爆炸和执行动态可行性及安全硬约束的困难；现有基于扩散模型的方法虽表现良好，但依赖大型演示数据集且难以严格执行动力学和安全约束。

Method: 引入MDOC，一种基于模型的扩散规划器，利用已知动力学模型和控制屏障函数约束投影作为安全机制，并通过冲突基础搜索自然扩展到多机器人场景，无需依赖数据即可生成动态可行轨迹。

Result: 模拟实验显示，MDOC在样本效率、几何平滑性、成功率方面优于代表性基线规划器，同时减少计算时间并生成严格无碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: MDOC提供了一种高效的数据无关解决方案，通过模型驱动方法可靠地生成安全、可行的多机器人轨迹，克服了现有扩散规划器的限制，并在多个关键指标上实现显著改进。

Abstract: Multi-Robot Motion Planning in continuous environments, where robots must generate dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories, is challenging due to the combinatorial growth of the joint trajectory space and the difficulty of enforcing dynamic feasibility and hard safety constraints. Recent approaches recast trajectory planning as probabilistic inference, sampling from a posterior over trajectories using diffusion models whose score functions are learned from demonstration data. While showing promising performance, these approaches are limited: they often rely on sizable demonstration datasets and struggle to rigorously enforce dynamics and hard safety constraints during sampling. To this end, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control (MDOC), a model-based diffusion planner that efficiently produces dynamically feasible trajectories without relying on data. Crucially, we show that MDOC's safety mechanism -- combining known dynamics models with Control Barrier Function-constrained projections -- naturally scales to multi-robot planning settings through Conflict-Based Search. Across simulation experiments, this integrated method consistently outperforms representative baseline planners in sample efficiency, geometric smoothness, and success rate, while reducing computation time and producing collision-free trajectories.

</details>


### [224] [Deployable Human Preference Alignment in Robotics: Learning Representative Rewards from Diverse Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12466)
*Taehyung Kim,Gwangmo Lee,Minjun Chang,Sunghyun Lim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Aligning robot policies with human preferences is essential for deployment to diverse end users. In per-user alignment approach, preference feedback is often sparse, so learning becomes unstable and vulnerable to human preference noise, and a growing number of individualized policies makes validation difficult before deployment. A single shared policy approach to user alignment avoids this cost but fails to capture heterogeneous preferences and often neglects minority preferences. To address these challenges, we introduce Preference-based REward Clustering (PREC), a novel framework that learns a compact set of policies from binary preference labels provided by diverse users. From a dataset of user trajectories and their preference labels, PREC first sets the labels aside and aggregates trajectories across users to learn a population-level shared trajectory encoder, alleviating limited per-user coverage and avoiding label noise during representation learning. Using this representation, PREC jointly assigns users to preference-coherent clusters and learns a representative reward model per cluster using preference labels, from which a policy is optimized for each cluster. Clustering similar users compensates for the limited number of labels available from each user and mitigates the effect of label noise. At the same time, maintaining a manageable number of reward models reduces the validation burden at deployment. Experiments across diverse simulated locomotion environments show that PREC groups users who label different trajectory subsets into preference-coherent clusters more accurately than baseline methods. Under sparse and noisy feedback, policies trained with PREC improve all three social welfare metrics over an existing single shared-policy user-alignment approach and even outperform per-user alignment approaches.

</details>


### [225] [Infra-Swarm: Robust Vision-Based Multi-Robot Swarming via Near-Infrared Spectral Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12489)
*Haoyu Chen,Qijin Li,Wanyu Xiang,Xiuxiu Lin,Zian Ning,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Infra-Swarm系统通过近红外光源与灰度相机实现厘米级3D位置感知，硬件级抗光照干扰（拒绝99.2%环境光），计算开销极低，支持资源受限硬件上的大规模机器人集群扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式群体依赖无线通信或被动视觉，易受带宽约束和环境敏感性影响，亟需一种鲁棒性强的替代方案以提升系统在复杂环境中的可靠性。

Method: 每个机器人集成940 nm近红外光源和四个普通灰度相机，利用窄带滤波器物理隔离环境光，通过捕获图像中光焰的方位（bearing）和强度（strength）直接解算邻居的厘米级3D位置，避免复杂算法处理。

Result: 系统实现厘米级定位精度，硬件级抑制99.2%环境光干扰，显著降低计算负载，验证了其在动态光照条件下的稳定性能。

Conclusion: Infra-Swarm为资源受限场景下的机器人集群提供了可扩展、鲁棒的感知基础，有效克服了传统方法的局限性，推动大规模分布式系统实用化。

Abstract: Distributed swarms typically rely on either active wireless communication or passive vision, and they are frequently hindered by bandwidth constraints or environmental sensitivity. This paper proposes Infra-Swarm, a robust vision-based swarm. Each robot is equipped with a near-infrared light source and four ordinary gray-scale cameras. The Infra-Swarm system directly measures the centimeter-level 3D position of neighbors based on the position (bearing) and intensity (strength) of optical flares in the captured images. By utilizing 940 nm narrow-band filters to physically reject 99.2% of ambient light interference, the perception front-end achieves hardware-level robustness against illumination variations. Furthermore, its minimal computational overhead provides a resilient foundation for the massive scalability of robotic collectives on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [226] [A Bearing-Strength Method for Motion Estimation of Unknown Energy Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12515)
*Haoyu Chen,Zian Ning,Yin Zhang,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出结合方位与接收信号强度的运动估计新方法，突破横向运动约束限制，实验验证有效且无额外成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方位-仅方位角运动估计方法需传感器具备横向运动以确保可观测性，但该要求常与任务目标冲突；接收信号强度易获取却未被充分利用。
